[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");

[ASSISTANT]
I have a clear picture. Let me lay out my plan:

**Plan:**
1. Baseline: copy `starting_point.v` → `design.sv`, evaluate for correctness + cost.
2. Optimized rewrite: the starting point uses 3 barrel shifters, an O(n²) sticky network, and a 22-deep priority mux. My design will:
   - Compute operand CLZs (la, lb) on the *input* side (parallel with the multiply); only 1 product bit (`d = ~prod[21-la-lb]`) is needed post-multiply to get the exact leading-zero count (`lz = la+lb+d`).
   - Share a **single** funnel barrel shifter (with sticky accumulator) between the normal path (`sh = 11-lz`) and subnormal path (`sh = 26-s`) via `sh = max(11-lz, 26-s)`.
   - Use one shared rounding incrementer (guard/sticky/lsb formula is identical for both paths).
3. Evaluate, then iterate on structural optimizations (control precompute, shifter narrowing, target_delay).

Let me start with the baseline evaluation and simultaneously write my optimized version:
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 floating-point multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction)\n// Subnormals supported, round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// Optimized structure:\n//   * operand CLZs are computed on the input side (parallel to the multiply);\n//     only one product bit (d = ~prod[21-la-lb]) fixes the exact product CLZ:\n//     lz = la + lb + d\n//   * ONE funnel barrel shifter (with sticky accumulator) serves both the\n//     normal path (shift 11-lz) and the subnormal path (shift 26-s):\n//     sh = max(11-lz, 26-s)\n//   * one shared rounding incrementer: round = guard & (sticky | lsb) is the\n//     same formula for normal and subnormal results\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n  wire [6:0] s_sum  = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};   // 2..60\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------- input-side CLZ of significands\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n  wire [4:0] t  = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};                    // 0..22\n  wire [4:0] k  = (t >= 5'd21) ? 5'd0 : (5'd21 - t);          // index into prod\n\n  // One product bit fixes the exact leading-zero count:  lz = t + d, d = ~prod[k]\n  wire [21:0] bp = prod;\n  wire [11:0] v1;\n  wire [7:0]  v2;\n  wire [3:0]  v3;\n  wire [1:0]  v4;\n  wire        v5;\n  genvar gi;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 11; gi = gi + 1) begin : g0\n      assign v1[gi] = k[0] ? bp[2*gi+1] : bp[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v1[11] = 1'b0;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 6; gi = gi + 1) begin : g1\n      assign v2[gi] = k[1] ? v1[2*gi+1] : v1[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v2[7:6] = 2'b00;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 3; gi = gi + 1) begin : g2\n      assign v3[gi] = k[2] ? v2[2*gi+1] : v2[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v3[3] = 1'b0;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 2; gi = gi + 1) begin : g3\n      assign v4[gi] = k[3] ? v3[2*gi+1] : v3[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v5 = k[4] ? v4[1] : v4[0];\n  endgenerate\n  wire d = ~v5;                       // lz = t + d\n\n  // -------------------------------------------------- exponent / shift control\n  // u = s_sum - lz + 32;  normal result iff E_field = u-46 >= 1\n  wire [6:0] u      = s_sum + 7'd32 - {2'b00, t} - {6'd0, d};\n  wire       is_norm = (u >= 7'd47);\n  wire [4:0] sh      = is_norm ? (5'd11 - t - {4'd0, d}) : (5'd26 - s_sum[4:0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------- single funnel shifter + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w  = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] q0 = sh[0] ? {1'b0,     w[22:1]}  : w;\n  wire [22:0] q1 = sh[1] ? {2'b00,    q0[22:2]} : q0;\n  wire [22:0] q2 = sh[2] ? {4'b0,     q1[22:4]} : q1;\n  wire [22:0] q3 = sh[3] ? {8'b0,     q2[22:8]} : q2;\n  wire [22:0] q4 = sh[4] ? {16'b0,    q3[22:16]} : q3;\n\n  wire sk1 = sh[1] & (|q0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (sh[2] & (|q1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (sh[3] & (|q2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (sh[4] & (|q3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = q4[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = q4[0];\n  wire        sticky   = sk4;\n  wire        rnd      = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n  wire [11:0] sum      = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, rnd};\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- pack\n  wire [6:0] e7        = u + {6'd0, sum[11]} - 7'd46;   // normal exponent field\n  wire       ovf       = (e7 > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm  = e7[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub   = sum[10] ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y    = sum[9:0];\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00 :\n             inf_in  ? {s_y, 5'h1F, 10'h0} :\n             zero_in ? {s_y, 15'h0} :\n             is_norm ? (ovf ? {s_y, 5'h1F, 10'h0} : {s_y, exp_norm, frac_y}) :\n                       {s_y, exp_sub, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Baseline comparison: my v1 design already improves ADP (133376 vs 145429) — area down 13%, but delay slightly worse. The critical path goes through the multiplier → d-bit select → shift-control arithmetic → shifter → rounding adder → pack.

**V2 strategy:** Move ALL control arithmetic (exponent sums, shift amount candidates, overflow flags) to the *input side* (parallel with the multiply), so only one product bit `d` and the rounding carry feed post-multiply muxes. Also decompose `sh = S_base - delta` so the 5-stage shifter has *early* control, with only a final 1-bit late-controlled stage. Let me write it:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v2: all control arithmetic is done on the INPUT side (parallel to the\n// multiply).  Post-multiply logic is only:\n//   d      = ~prod[21-t]            (one bit of the product)\n//   delta  = gt & d                 (1 gate)\n//   the funnel shifter with EARLY control (S_pre), plus one late 1-bit stage\n//   the rounding incrementer and 2 small output muxes\n//\n// Math:  lz = la + lb + d  (d in {0,1});  E_field = s - lz - 14\n//        sh = max(11 - lz, 26 - s) = S_base - delta\n//          S_base = gt ? (11-t) : (26-s),  delta = gt & d\n//        normal result iff E_field >= 1;  same round formula for both paths:\n//        round = guard & (sticky | lsb)\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals is always < half of the min subnormal -> signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ------------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[k]\n  wire [4:0] k = (t >= 5'd21) ? 5'd0 : (5'd21 - t);\n  wire [21:0] bp = prod;\n  wire [11:0] v1;\n  wire [7:0]  v2;\n  wire [3:0]  v3;\n  wire [1:0]  v4;\n  wire        v5;\n  genvar gi;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 11; gi = gi + 1) begin : g0\n      assign v1[gi] = k[0] ? bp[2*gi+1] : bp[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v1[11] = 1'b0;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 6; gi = gi + 1) begin : g1\n      assign v2[gi] = k[1] ? v1[2*gi+1] : v1[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v2[7:6] = 2'b00;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 3; gi = gi + 1) begin : g2\n      assign v3[gi] = k[2] ? v2[2*gi+1] : v2[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v3[3] = 1'b0;\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 2; gi = gi + 1) begin : g3\n      assign v4[gi] = k[3] ? v3[2*gi+1] : v3[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign v5 = k[4] ? v4[1] : v4[0];\n  endgenerate\n  wire d = ~v5;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- shared round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = q[0];\n  wire        rnd      = guard & (sk | mant_pre[0]);\n  wire [11:0] sum      = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, rnd};\n  wire        carry    = sum[11];\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? EP[4:0] : EM[4:0]) : E0v[4:0];\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP     : ovM    ) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = sum[10] ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y   = sum[9:0];\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- pack\n  assign y = nan_in   ? 16'h7E00 :\n             inf_in   ? {s_y, 5'h1F, 10'h0} :\n             zero_in  ? {s_y, 15'h0} :\n             both_sub ? {s_y, 15'h0} :\n             is_norm  ? (ovf ? {s_y, 5'h1F, 10'h0} : {s_y, exp_norm, frac_y}) :\n                        {s_y, exp_sub, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v2.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v2.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V2 passes with ADP **103731** (delay 1112ps) — a 22% improvement. The critical path is: multiplier (~450ps) → d-bit select tree → shifter → rounding incrementer → output pack mux chain.

**V3 strategy:**
1. Compact the d-select tree to an 11:1 mux (in all valid cases `k = 21-t ∈ [11,21]`; other cases are already special-cased) — saves a level + area.
2. Replace the 5-way priority pack chain with **3 parallel mux trees** (sign/exp/frac computed independently).
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v3 = v2 + compact 11:1 product-bit select + parallel output packing.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // valid (non-special) cases have t <= 10, so index 21-t in [11,21]\n  wire [3:0] kk = (t > 5'd10) ? 4'd0 : (4'd10 - t[3:0]);\n  wire [15:0] src = {5'b0, prod[21:11]};\n  wire [7:0] a0;\n  wire [3:0] a1;\n  wire [1:0] a2;\n  wire       a3;\n  genvar gi;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 8; gi = gi + 1) begin : g0\n      assign a0[gi] = kk[0] ? src[2*gi+1] : src[2*gi];\n    end\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 4; gi = gi + 1) begin : g1\n      assign a1[gi] = kk[1] ? a0[2*gi+1] : a0[2*gi];\n    end\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 2; gi = gi + 1) begin : g2\n      assign a2[gi] = kk[2] ? a1[2*gi+1] : a1[2*gi];\n    end\n    assign a3 = kk[3] ? a2[1] : a2[0];\n  endgenerate\n  wire d = ~a3;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- shared round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = q[0];\n  wire        rnd      = guard & (sk | mant_pre[0]);\n  wire [11:0] sum      = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, rnd};\n  wire        carry    = sum[11];\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? EP[4:0] : EM[4:0]) : E0v[4:0];\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP     : ovM    ) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = sum[10] ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? (ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_norm) : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in)  ? 5'd31 :\n                     (zero_in | both_sub) ? 5'd0 : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in             ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | zero_in | both_sub) ? 10'h0 : sum[9:0];\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v3.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v3.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V3 passes at ADP 102855; relaxed target is worse (109507), so aggressive mapping is right. The dominant cost is multiplier → **d-bit extraction tree** → shifter. 

**V4 — key structural insight:** normalize the *operands* instead of extracting a product bit at a variable index. Let `A' = m_a << la`, `B' = m_b << lb` (input-side, parallel to multiply), so `P' = A'·B' = P·2^t` exactly (low `t` bits are zero!). Then:
- `d = ~P'[21]` — the **top bit of the product**, no extraction tree at all!
- The result window shifts by `sh' = sh + t = max(11-d, 58-u0)` — no dependence on `t` either.
- Sticky semantics are preserved since `P'[t-1:0] == 0`.
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v4 = v3 with normalized operands:\n//   A' = m_a << la,  B' = m_b << lb   (input side, parallel to the multiply)\n//   P' = A' * B' = P * 2^(la+lb)      (low la+lb bits of P' are zero)\n//   => d = ~P'[21] is just the TOP product bit (no variable-index extraction!)\n//   => window shift sh' = sh + t = max(11 - d, 58 - u0), no t dependence.\n// Sticky unchanged: |P'[sh'-2:0] = |P[sh-2:0] since P'[t-1:0] = 0.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------- normalized significands (input side)\n  wire [10:0] ma_s0 = la[0] ? {m_a[9:0],  1'b0}  : m_a;\n  wire [10:0] ma_s1 = la[1] ? {ma_s0[7:0], 3'b0} : ma_s0;\n  wire [10:0] ma_s2 = la[2] ? {ma_s1[3:0], 7'b0} : ma_s1;\n  wire [10:0] A     = la[3] ? {ma_s2[2:0], 8'b0} : ma_s2;\n  wire [10:0] mb_s0 = lb[0] ? {m_b[9:0],  1'b0}  : m_b;\n  wire [10:0] mb_s1 = lb[1] ? {mb_s0[7:0], 3'b0} : mb_s0;\n  wire [10:0] mb_s2 = lb[2] ? {mb_s1[3:0], 7'b0} : mb_s1;\n  wire [10:0] B     = lb[3] ? {mb_s2[2:0], 8'b0} : mb_s2;\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = A * B;          // = m_a*m_b << (la+lb)\n  wire        d    = ~prod[21];      // lz(prod_unshifted) = la+lb+d\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [5:0] pa = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0 = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - (la+lb) + 32\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  // sh' = max(11-d, 58-u0) = S_pre + L,  S_pre = S_base-1, L = ~delta\n  wire [5:0] sp6  = gt ? 6'd10 : (6'd57 - u0[5:0]);\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = (sp6 > 6'd31) ? 5'd31 : sp6[4:0];\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- shared round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = q[0];\n  wire        rnd      = guard & (sk | mant_pre[0]);\n  wire [11:0] sum      = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, rnd};\n  wire        carry    = sum[11];\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : EM[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : EP[4:0];\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? SP : SM) : S0;\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = sum[10] ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire z = zero_in | both_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? exp_norm : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     z                  ? 5'd0  : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in           ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | z | (is_norm & ovf)) ? 10'h0 : sum[9:0];\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v4.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v4.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V4 passes but is **worse** (129524) — the normalization shifters + CLZ moved ~240ps *in front of* the multiplier. Reverting to v3's multiply structure. But I learned useful things. **V5 plan** (on v3's skeleton):
1. `d = ~|{prod[21:11] & thermometer(t)}` — 3-level AND-OR instead of the 4-level 11:1 mux tree (valid since `prod < 2^(22-t)`).
2. Math simplification: since `A[i-1] = &mp[i-1:0]` implies `mp[0]=1`, the sticky term **drops out of every carry except bit 0**: `frac[i] = mp[i] ^ (guard & &mp[i-1:0])`, `carry = guard & &mp[10:0]` — an explicit shared grouped-AND prefix incrementer.
3. Saturated exponent candidates → 2-level output muxes.
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v5 = v3 structure with a faster tail:\n//   * d = ~|{prod[21:11] & thermometer(t)}   (3-level AND-OR, was 4-level mux)\n//   * rounding carries via shared grouped-AND prefix; sticky term drops out\n//     of all carries except bit 0:  frac[i] = mp[i] ^ (guard & &mp[i-1:0])\n//   * saturated exponent candidates -> 2-level output muxes\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // prod < 2^(22-t), so bits prod[21:22-t] are zero and\n  // d = ~|prod[21:21-t] = ~|{prod[21:11] & M} with thermometer mask M(t).\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  // A[i] = &mp[i:0]; carries = guard & A[i-1]  (sticky only affects bit 0)\n  wire [10:0] mp   = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  wire g01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire g23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire g45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire g67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire g89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n  wire A3  = g01 & g23;\n  wire A7  = A3 & g45 & g67;\n  wire A9  = A7 & g89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = g01;\n  wire A2  = g01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3 & g45;\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : EM[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : EP[4:0];\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? SP : SM) : S0;\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = carry_s ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire z = zero_in | both_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? exp_norm : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     z                  ? 5'd0  : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | z | (is_norm & ovf)) ? 10'h0 : frac;\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v5.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v5.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V5 passes: ADP **94422** (994.7ps) — another 8% down. The tail is now a nested mux chain in the exponent path (an OR5 cell at 60ps shows ABC struggling). **V6: flatten the entire output stage into 2-level AND-OR form**, with all constants (exponent candidates, saturated overflow values) arriving early from the input side, and simplify the weight terms using `selX & carry = ~d & carry`:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v6 = v5 with a completely flat 2-level AND-OR output stage: all output bits\n// are  w31 | (wSP & SP) | (wSM & SM) | (wS0 & S0) | (c_s & carry_s)  etc.,\n// where SP/SM/S0 are early input-side constants and the weights depend only\n// on d, carry, gt, eq and the early special-case flags.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // prod < 2^(22-t) so bits prod[21:22-t] are zero and\n  // d = ~|prod[21:21-t] = ~|{prod[21:11] & M} with thermometer mask M(t).\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  wire g01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire g23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire g45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire g67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire g89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n  wire A3  = g01 & g23;\n  wire A7  = A3 & g45 & g67;\n  wire A9  = A7 & g89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = g01;\n  wire A2  = g01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3 & g45;\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ((u0 - 7'd46) > 7'd30) ? 5'd31 : (u0 - 7'd46);\n  wire [4:0] SM = ((u0 - 7'd47) > 7'd30) ? 5'd31 : (u0 - 7'd47);\n  wire [4:0] SP = ((u0 - 7'd45) > 7'd30) ? 5'd31 : (u0 - 7'd45);\n  wire ov0 = ((u0 - 7'd46) > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = ((u0 - 7'd47) > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = ((u0 - 7'd45) > 7'd30);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------ flat output stage\n  wire sp  = nan_in | inf_in;                    // -> exponent 31\n  wire z   = zero_in | both_sub;                 // -> signed zero\n  wire g_n = gt & ~sp & ~z;\n  wire e_n = eq & ~sp & ~z;\n  wire c_s = ~sp & ~z & ~gt & (~eq | d);         // subnormal result\n\n  // weights (selected exponent candidate)\n  wire wSM = g_n & d & ~carry;\n  wire wSP = (g_n | e_n) & ~d & carry;\n  wire wS0 = ((g_n | e_n) & ~d & ~carry) | (g_n & d & carry);\n\n  wire ovn = (wS0 & ov0) | (wSP & ovP) | (wSM & ovM);   // overflow -> inf\n  wire wF  = ~sp & ~z & ~ovn;                          // frac passes\n\n  assign y[15] = ~nan_in & s_y;\n  assign y[14] = sp | (wSP & SP[4]) | (wSM & SM[4]) | (wS0 & S0[4]);\n  assign y[13] = sp | (wSP & SP[3]) | (wSM & SM[3]) | (wS0 & S0[3]);\n  assign y[12] = sp | (wSP & SP[2]) | (wSM & SM[2]) | (wS0 & S0[2]);\n  assign y[11] = sp | (wSP & SP[1]) | (wSM & SM[1]) | (wS0 & S0[1]);\n  assign y[10] = sp | (wSP & SP[0]) | (wSM & SM[0]) | (wS0 & S0[0]) | (c_s & carry_s);\n  assign y[9]  = nan_in | (wF & frac[9]);\n  assign y[8]  = wF & frac[8];\n  assign y[7]  = wF & frac[7];\n  assign y[6]  = wF & frac[6];\n  assign y[5]  = wF & frac[5];\n  assign y[4]  = wF & frac[4];\n  assign y[3]  = wF & frac[3];\n  assign y[2]  = wF & frac[2];\n  assign y[1]  = wF & frac[1];\n  assign y[0]  = wF & frac[0];\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v6.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v6.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V6 is worse (100768) — the flat form made the *fraction* path critical (wF/ovn cone with slow wide gates). Lesson: v5's nested mux form mapped better. **V7: best of both** — keep v5 exactly, but flatten *only the exponent side* (which was v5's critical path), keeping v5's fraction-select form:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v7 = v5 (best so far) with only the exponent side flattened to a 2-level\n// AND-OR of early constants; fraction side keeps v5's select form.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // prod < 2^(22-t) so bits prod[21:22-t] are zero and\n  // d = ~|prod[21:21-t] = ~|{prod[21:11] & M} with thermometer mask M(t).\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  wire g01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire g23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire g45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire g67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire g89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n  wire A3  = g01 & g23;\n  wire A7  = A3 & g45 & g67;\n  wire A9  = A7 & g89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = g01;\n  wire A2  = g01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3 & g45;\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (E0v > 7'd31);\n  wire ovP = (E0v > 7'd29);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : (E0v[4:0] - 5'd1);\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : (E0v[4:0] + 5'd1);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- exponent side (flat, 2 level)\n  wire sp  = nan_in | inf_in;                    // -> exponent 31\n  wire z   = zero_in | both_sub;                 // -> signed zero\n  wire g_n = gt & ~sp & ~z;\n  wire e_n = eq & ~sp & ~z;\n\n  wire wSM = g_n & d & ~carry;\n  wire wSP = (g_n | e_n) & ~d & carry;\n  wire wS0 = ((g_n | e_n) & ~d & ~carry) | (g_n & d & carry);\n\n  assign y[14] = sp | (wSP & SP[4]) | (wSM & SM[4]) | (wS0 & S0[4]);\n  assign y[13] = sp | (wSP & SP[3]) | (wSM & SM[3]) | (wS0 & S0[3]);\n  assign y[12] = sp | (wSP & SP[2]) | (wSM & SM[2]) | (wS0 & S0[2]);\n  assign y[11] = sp | (wSP & SP[1]) | (wSM & SM[1]) | (wS0 & S0[1]);\n  assign y[10] = sp | (wSP & SP[0]) | (wSM & SM[0]) | (wS0 & S0[0])\n                   | ((c_s_w) & carry_s);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- fraction side (v5 form)\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire ovf  = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire is_norm = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire c_s_w = ~sp & ~z & ~is_norm;              // subnormal result\n  wire wF = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~z & ~(is_norm & ovf);\n\n  assign y[15] = ~nan_in & s_y;\n  assign y[9]  = nan_in | (wF & frac[9]);\n  assign y[8]  = wF & frac[8];\n  assign y[7]  = wF & frac[7];\n  assign y[6]  = wF & frac[6];\n  assign y[5]  = wF & frac[5];\n  assign y[4]  = wF & frac[4];\n  assign y[3]  = wF & frac[3];\n  assign y[2]  = wF & frac[2];\n  assign y[1]  = wF & frac[1];\n  assign y[0]  = wF & frac[0];\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v7.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V7 (97435) also misses v5 — ABC remapping noise dominates when I change too much at once. Back to **v5 as base**, with ONE targeted change: my explicit AND-prefix (`A3→A7→A9→A10`) forces a 5-deep *chain*. Writing the same functions as **balanced multi-AND groups** (AND4/AND3 cells = 1 level each) cuts ~2 levels from both the exponent and fraction paths:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v8 = v5 with the rounding carry prefix written as balanced multi-AND groups\n// (wide AND cells, ~3 levels) instead of a chained prefix (~5 levels).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // prod < 2^(22-t) so bits prod[21:22-t] are zero and\n  // d = ~|prod[21:21-t] = ~|{prod[21:11] & M} with thermometer mask M(t).\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  // balanced AND groups\n  wire c01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire c23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire c45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire c67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire c89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n\n  wire A1  = c01;\n  wire A2  = c01 & mp[2];\n  wire A3  = c01 & c23;\n  wire A4  = A3  & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3  & c45;\n  wire A6  = A5  & mp[6];\n  wire A7  = {c01, c23, c45, c67} == 4'b1111 ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;\n  wire A8  = A7  & c67;\n  wire A9  = A7  & c89;\n  wire A10 = A9  & mp[10];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A8);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A9);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : EM[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : EP[4:0];\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? SP : SM) : S0;\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = carry_s ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire z = zero_in | both_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? exp_norm : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     z                  ? 5'd0  : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | z | (is_norm & ovf)) ? 10'h0 : frac;\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v8.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v8.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V8 passes but is slower (1073ps) — ABC mapping variance flips which cone is critical (now a slow AOI311/NAND2x2 in the *fraction-zeroing* cone). Best is still v5. **V9: v5 + one cheap, predictable win** — the fraction zeroing (overflow→inf) can use `is_norm & (exp_norm == 5'd31)` (since real finite results never have exponent field 31 — saturation guarantees it), replacing the deep `ovf` mux cone with a 5-bit AND:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v9 = v5 with a cheaper fraction-zeroing cone: overflow-to-inf is detected\n// as is_norm & (exp_norm == 31) (finite results never have exp field 31;\n// candidates are saturated), instead of a separate mux cone over ov flags.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ?
