4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? [ASSISTANT]
Target 300 gives an *identical* netlist — the mapping is already at max speed; targets only matter near the achievable delay. So further gains must come from structural luck or real area cuts. Two safe observations: (1) the `both_sub` special case is **provable unnecessary** (the general path yields signed zero: `gt=0, eq=0` there regardless of the garbage `d`), and (2) small netlist perturbations reshuffle ABC. Let me try v15 = v5 minus `both_sub`, plus a couple of safe tidies:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v15 = v5 minus the both_sub special case (general path already yields a\n// signed zero there: gt=0, eq=0 so d is unused), minor pack tidies.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // -------------------------- funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [22:0] w   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [22:0] qa0 = S_pre[0] ? {1'b0,  w[22:1]}   : w;\n  wire [22:0] qa1 = S_pre[1] ? {2'b00, qa0[22:2]} : qa0;\n  wire [22:0] qa2 = S_pre[2] ? {4'b0,  qa1[22:4]} : qa1;\n  wire [22:0] qa3 = S_pre[3] ? {8'b0,  qa2[22:8]} : qa2;\n  wire [22:0] qa4 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, qa3[22:16]}: qa3;\n\n  wire sk1 = S_pre[1] & (|qa0[1:0]);\n  wire sk2 = sk1 | (S_pre[2] & (|qa1[3:0]));\n  wire sk3 = sk2 | (S_pre[3] & (|qa2[7:0]));\n  wire sk4 = sk3 | (S_pre[4] & (|qa3[15:0]));\n\n  wire [22:0] q  = L ? {1'b0, qa4[22:1]} : qa4;\n  wire        sk = sk4 | (L & qa4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  wire g01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire g23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire g45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire g67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire g89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n  wire A3  = g01 & g23;\n  wire A7  = A3 & g45 & g67;\n  wire A9  = A7 & g89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = g01;\n  wire A2  = g01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3 & g45;\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : EM[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : EP[4:0];\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? SP : SM) : S0;\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = {4'b0, carry_s};\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire z = zero_in;\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? exp_norm : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     z                  ? 5'd0  : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in ? 10'h200 :\n                      ((is_norm & ovf) | inf_in | z) ? 10'h0 : frac;\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v15.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v15.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
V15 also misses (99984). Mapping luck dominates; but I found one **real, predictable area lever**: the 23-bit×5-stage shifter can be narrowed to a truncating funnel (keep-widths 28/20/16/14/13 for stage order 16,8,4,2,1) — 103 muxes vs 138 with identical depth and semantics. **V16 = v5 + narrowed shifter**:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v16 = v5 with the funnel shifter narrowed (truncating stages, big shifts\n// first): 103 muxes instead of 138, same 6 mux levels, same semantics.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,          (t >= 5'd1),  (t >= 5'd2),\n                        (t >= 5'd3),  (t >= 5'd4),  (t >= 5'd5),\n                        (t >= 5'd6),  (t >= 5'd7),  (t >= 5'd8),\n                        (t >= 5'd9),  (t >= 5'd10)};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // ----------------------- narrow funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  // keep-widths 28/20/16/14/13 for shifts 16/8/4/2/1; last 1-bit stage is L.\n  wire [27:0] wx  = {5'b0, prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [27:0] v0 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, wx[27:16]} : wx;\n  wire        s0 = S_pre[4] & (|wx[15:0]);\n  wire [19:0] v1 = S_pre[3] ? v0[27:8] : v0[19:0];\n  wire        s1 = s0 | (S_pre[3] & (|v0[7:0]));\n  wire [15:0] v2 = S_pre[2] ? v1[19:4] : v1[15:0];\n  wire        s2 = s1 | (S_pre[2] & (|v1[3:0]));\n  wire [13:0] v3 = S_pre[1] ? v2[15:2] : v2[13:0];\n  wire        s3 = s2 | (S_pre[1] & (|v2[1:0]));\n  wire [12:0] v4 = S_pre[0] ? v3[13:1] : v3[12:0];\n  wire        s4 = s3 | (S_pre[0] & v3[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] q12 = L ? v4[12:1] : v4[11:0];\n  wire        sk  = s4 | (L & v4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q12[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q12[0];\n  wire        rnd   = guard & (sk | mp[0]);      // carry into bit 0 only\n\n  wire g01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire g23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire g45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire g67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire g89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n  wire A3  = g01 & g23;\n  wire A7  = A3 & g45 & g67;\n  wire A9  = A7 & g89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = g01;\n  wire A2  = g01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A5  = A3 & g45;\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire [6:0] EM  = u0 - 7'd47;\n  wire [6:0] EP  = u0 - 7'd45;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v > 7'd30);\n  wire ovM = (EM  > 7'd30);\n  wire ovP = (EP  > 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : EM[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : EP[4:0];\n\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = selX ? (carry ? SP : SM) : S0;\n  wire       ovf      = selX ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire       is_norm  = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [4:0] exp_sub  = carry_s ? 5'd1 : 5'd0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- parallel output pack\n  wire z = zero_in | both_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_data = is_norm ? exp_norm : exp_sub;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     z                  ? 5'd0  : exp_data;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | z | (is_norm & ovf)) ? 10'h0 : frac;\n  wire       sign_y = nan_in ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v16.sv')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design_v16.sv')

6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // thermometer decode of t (2-level), Mv[j] = (t >= 10-j)\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // ----------------------- narrow funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [27:0] wx  = {5'b0, prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [27:0] v0 = S_pre[4] ? [ASSISTANT]
V20 sets the **lowest area yet (90.16)** but the draw's tail is long. The rounding AND-prefix `A3→A7→A9→A10` is a 5-deep chain on the critical frac path — balancing it (`A7 = AND4(c's)`) cuts a full level. **V21 = v20 + balanced prefix**:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v21 = v20 with the rounding carry prefix balanced (AND4 groups instead of\n// a 4-deep chain): one level off the fraction/exponent critical paths.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // thermometer decode of t (2-level), Mv[j] = (t >= 10-j)\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // ----------------------- narrow funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [27:0] wx  = {5'b0, prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [27:0] v0 = S_pre[4] ? {16'b0, wx[27:16]} : wx;\n  wire [19:0] v1 = S_pre[3] ? v0[27:8] : v0[19:0];\n  wire [15:0] v2 = S_pre[2] ? v1[19:4] : v1[15:0];\n  wire [13:0] v3 = S_pre[1] ? v2[15:2] : v2[13:0];\n  wire [12:0] v4 = S_pre[0] ? v3[13:1] : v3[12:0];\n\n  wire [11:0] q12 = L ? v4[12:1] : v4[11:0];\n\n  wire sk = (S_pre[4] & (|wx[15:0])) | (S_pre[3] & (|v0[7:0])) |\n            (S_pre[2] & (|v1[3:0]))  | (S_pre[1] & (|v2[1:0])) |\n            (S_pre[0] & v3[0])       | (L & v4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q12[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q12[0];\n  wire        rnd   = (guard & sk) | (guard & mp[0]);  // carry into bit 0\n\n  // balanced AND groups: c's 1 level, A7/A8/A9 ~2-3 levels\n  wire c01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire c23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire c45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire c67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire c89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n\n  wire A7  = c01 & c23 & c45 & c67;\n  wire A3  = c01 & c23;\n  wire A5  = A3 & c45;\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n  wire A9  = A7 & c89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = c01;\n  wire A2  = c01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v >= 7'd31);\n  wire ovM = (E0v >= 7'd32);\n  wire ovP = (E0v >= 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? (carry ? ovP : ovM) : ov0;\n  wire is_norm = gt | (eq & ~d);\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = nan_in ? 10'h200 :\n                      (inf_in | z | (is_norm & ovf)) ? 10'h0 : frac;\n\n  assign y[15] = ~nan_in & s_y;\n  assign y[9]  = frac_y[9];\n  assign y[8]  = frac_y[8];\n  assign y[7]  = frac_y[7];\n  assign y[6]  = frac_y[6];\n  assign y[5]  = frac_y[5];\n  assign y[4]  = frac_y[4];\n  assign y[3]  = frac_y[3];\n  assign y[2]  = frac_y[2];\n  assign y[1]  = frac_y[1];\n  assign y[0]  = frac_y[0];\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v21.sv')

4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // thermometer decode of t (2-level), Mv[j] = (t >= 10-j)\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // ----------------------- narrow funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [27:0] wx  = {5'b0, prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [27:0] v0 = S_pre[4] ? v3[13:1] : v3[12:0];\n\n  wire [11:0] q12 = L ? v4[12:1] : v4[11:0];\n\n  wire sk = (S_pre[4] & (|wx[15:0])) | (S_pre[3] & (|v0[7:0])) |\n            (S_pre[2] & (|v1[3:0]))  | (S_pre[1] & (|v2[1:0])) |\n            (S_pre[0] & v3[0])       | (L & v4[0]);\n\n  // --------------------------------------------- round / significand\n  wire [10:0] mp    = q12[11:1];\n  wire        guard = q12[0];\n  wire        rnd   = (guard & sk) | (guard & mp[0]);  // carry into bit 0\n\n  // balanced AND groups\n  wire c01 = mp[0] & mp[1];\n  wire c23 = mp[2] & mp[3];\n  wire c45 = mp[4] & mp[5];\n  wire c67 = mp[6] & mp[7];\n  wire c89 = mp[8] & mp[9];\n\n  wire A7  = c01 & c23 & c45 & c67;\n  wire A3  = c01 & c23;\n  wire A5  = A3 & c45;\n  wire A8  = A7 & mp[8];\n  wire A9  = A7 & c89;\n  wire A10 = A9 & mp[10];\n  wire A0  = mp[0];\n  wire A1  = c01;\n  wire A2  = c01 & mp[2];\n  wire A4  = A3 & mp[4];\n  wire A6  = A5 & mp[6];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac;\n  assign frac[0] = mp[0] ^ rnd;\n  assign frac[1] = mp[1] ^ (guard & A0);\n  assign frac[2] = mp[2] ^ (guard & A1);\n  assign frac[3] = mp[3] ^ (guard & A2);\n  assign frac[4] = mp[4] ^ (guard & A3);\n  assign frac[5] = mp[5] ^ (guard & A4);\n  assign frac[6] = mp[6] ^ (guard & A5);\n  assign frac[7] = mp[7] ^ (guard & A6);\n  assign frac[8] = mp[8] ^ (guard & A7);\n  assign frac[9] = mp[9] ^ (guard & A8);\n\n  wire carry   = guard & A10;                    // exp increment (normal)\n  wire carry_s = mp[10] ^ (guard & A9);          // subnormal -> min normal\n\n  // ------------------------------------- exponent candidates (input side)\n  wire [6:0] E0v = u0 - 7'd46;\n  wire ov0 = (E0v >= 7'd31);\n  wire ovM = (E0v >= 7'd32);\n  wire ovP = (E0v >= 7'd30);\n  wire [4:0] S0 = ov0 ? 5'd31 : E0v[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] SM = ovM ? 5'd31 : (E0v[4:0] - 5'd1);\n  wire [4:0] SP = ovP ? 5'd31 : (E0v[4:0] + 5'd1);\n\n  // ---------------------------- exponent output: weighted AND-OR (flat)\n  wire sp  = nan_in | inf_in;\n  wire z   = zero_in | both_sub;\n  wire g_n = gt & ~sp & ~z;\n  wire e_n = eq & ~sp & ~z;\n  wire wSM = g_n & d & ~carry;\n  wire wSP = (g_n | e_n) & ~d & carry;\n  wire wS0 = ((g_n | e_n) & ~d & ~carry) | (g_n & d & carry);\n  wire c_s = ~sp & ~z & ~gt & (~eq | d);\n\n  assign y[14] = sp | (wSP & SP[4]) | (wSM & SM[4]) | (wS0 & S0[4]);\n  assign y[13] = sp | (wSP & SP[3]) | (wSM & SM[3]) | (wS0 & S0[3]);\n  assign y[12] = sp | (wSP & SP[2]) | (wSM & SM[2]) | (wS0 & S0[2]);\n  assign y[11] = sp | (wSP & SP[1]) | (wSM & SM[1]) | (wS0 & S0[1]);\n  assign y[10] = sp | (wSP & SP[0]) | (wSM & SM[0]) | (wS0 & S0[0]) | (c_s & carry_s);\n\n  // ---------------------------------- fraction side: single AND gating\n  wire selX = d ^ carry;\n  wire ovf  = selX ? V23 (96853) — again a wide-cell-laden tail. Structural diagnosis: the flat exponent SOPs force 5-input cells (NOR5xp2 63ps, NAND5xp2 68ps) in the tail. The nested mux form uses only ≤4-input gates. **V24: keep v23's shared-weight overflow + AND-form fraction, but nested exponent muxes (no wide gates in the tail)**:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier (1 sign, 5 exp, 10 frac), subnormals,\n// round-to-nearest ties-to-even, canonical qNaN 16'h7E00.\n//\n// v24 = v23 with the exponent output in nested-mux form (only <=4-input\n// gates in the tail; the flat SOP form forces slow 5-input cells).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------- unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == 5'h1F);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == 5'h1F);\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd0 : v[9] ? 4'd1 : 4'd2;\n      else if (|v[7:5])  clz11 = v[7]  ? 4'd3 : v[6] ? 4'd4 : 4'd5;\n      else if (|v[4:2])  clz11 = v[4]  ? 4'd6 : v[3] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n      else               clz11 = v[1]  ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  // thermometer decode of t (2-level), Mv[j] = (t >= 10-j)\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? Am1 : Bm1;   // = S_base - 1\n  wire       delta = gt & d;\n  wire       L     = ~delta;           // late 1-bit extra shift\n\n  // ----------------------- narrow funnel shifter (early control) + sticky\n  wire [27:0] wx  = {5'b0, prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [27:0] v0 = S_pre[4] ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? [ASSISTANT]
**Critical pattern found!** Look at the draw history: every variant using the `E0v±1` candidate form (v20–v26) drew ≥1066ps, while every variant with the `EM/EP` subtractor form (v5, v16–v19) drew ≤1011ps — 7/7 vs 5/5. 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 4'd9 : v[0] ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    end\n  endfunction\n  wire [3:0] la = clz11(m_a);\n  wire [3:0] lb = clz11(m_b);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- input-side combined terms\n  wire [4:0] t     = {1'b0, la} + {1'b0, lb};        // 0..22\n  wire [5:0] s_sum = ea_eff + eb_eff;                // 2..60\n  wire [5:0] pa    = ea_eff - {2'b0, la} + 6'd16;    // 6..47\n  wire [5:0] pb    = eb_eff - {2'b0, lb} + 6'd16;\n  wire [6:0] u0    = {1'b0, pa} + {1'b0, pb};        // = s - t + 32, 12..94\n\n  wire gt = (u0 >= 7'd48);      // E_field >= 2 : normal for both d\n  wire eq = (u0 == 7'd47);      // E_field == 1 : normal iff d == 0\n\n  wire [4:0] Am1 = 5'd10 - t;          // (11 - t) - 1\n  wire [4:0] Bm1 = 5'd25 - s_sum[4:0]; // (26 - s) - 1\n\n  // ---------------------- one product bit: exact lz = t + d, d = ~prod[21-t]\n  wire t01 = t[1] & t[0];\n  wire t0i = t[1] | t[0];\n  wire lo2 = |t[2:0];\n  wire [10:0] Mv = {1'b1,\n                    |t[4:0],\n                    |t[4:1],\n                    |t[4:2] | t01,\n                    |t[4:2],\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t0i),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t[1]),\n                    |t[4:3] | (t[2] & t01),\n                    |t[4:3],\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & lo2),\n                    t[4] | (t[3] & (t[2] | t01))};\n  wire d = ~|(prod[21:11] & Mv);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------- shift control\n  wire [4:0] S_pre = gt ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_a};\n  wire [5:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 6'd1 : {1'b0, e_b};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // product of two subnormals always rounds to signed zero\n  wire both_sub = (e_a_zero & (|f_a)) & (e_b_zero & (|f_b));\n\n  // ------------------------------------------- operand CLZs (input side)\n  function [3:0] clz11;\n    input [10:0] v;\n    begin\n      if      (|v[10:8]) clz11 = v[10] ? ## Final Result

**`design_v41.sv`** — 100% correct (18,874,371/18,874,371 checks), **area_delay_product = 87 494** (delay 973.4 ps × area 89.89).

| Design | Delay | Area | ADP | vs. start |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| starting_point.v | 1338 ps | 108.69 | 145 429 | — |

## Architecture of the winning design

1. **Control precompute on the input side** (parallel with the multiply): operand CLZs (`la`, `lb`), combined `u0 = (s − t + 32)`, and the shift/exponent candidates — so only **one product bit** is needed after the multiplier.
2. **`d = ~|{prod[21:11] & thermometer(t)}`** — exploits `prod < 2^(22−t)` to replace a variable-index bit extraction with a 3-level AND-OR.
3. **Unified shift** `sh = max(11−lz, 26−s) = S_pre + L`: the 5 shifter stages are *early-controlled* (input side), with a single late 1-bit stage sitting on the short `d` path — the two paths balance.
4. **Narrow truncating funnel shifter** (keep-widths 28/20/16/14/13 → 103 muxes vs 138) with flat sticky accumulator.
5. **Rounding insight**: since `&mp[i-1:0] ⇒ mp[0]`, the sticky term vanishes from every carry except bit 0 — `frac[i] = mp[i] ^ (guard & &mp[i-1:0])`, `carry = guard & &mp[10:0]`, with a shared grouped-AND prefix and one shared rounding incrementer for normal *and* subnormal results.
6. **Flat weighted AND-OR exponent output** with saturated candidates (`S0/SM/SP`) as early constants; mux-form fraction gating; special cases (canonical qNaN `0x7E00`, inf/zero signs, overflow→inf, both-subnormal→signed zero) proven to fall out of the general path or muxed cheaply.

Systematic experiments established which knobs truly matter (flat exponent ≈ −130 ps, mask-OR `d`, unified shifter, merged `u0` arithmetic −3.4% area) versus which are normalized away by Yosys (AND-tree grouping, sticky/rnd forms) or harmful (AND-form fraction gating, `E0v±1` candidate form).

## Summary

**Final result:** `design_v41.sv` — PASS (18.87M/18.87M vectors), ADP **87 494** (973 ps × 89.89), vs. `starting_point.v` at 145 429 (1338 ps × 108.7) → **−40%**.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **v2** (`design_v2.sv`): precomputed all control (CLZs, exponent sums, shift candidates) on the input side, shared one funnel shifter + one rounding incrementer for normal/subnormal paths. Already −8% vs start.
- **v5** (`design_v5.sv`): mask-OR product bit `d = ~|{prod[21:11] & thermo(t)}` (from the identity `prod < 2^(22−t)`), sk-free rounding carries, unified shift `sh = S_pre + L` with early-controlled shifter stages + one late 1-bit stage. −36%.
- **v16/v17**: narrow truncating funnel shifter (103 vs 138 muxes) + flat weighted AND-OR exponent output.
- **v41** (winner): merged `u0 = s_sum + (32−t)` arithmetic (3 adds → 2), dropped the provably-redundant `both_sub` case, EM/EP subtractor candidates.
- Also tried normalized-operand multiplier (v4, to make `d` the product top bit), flat output packing, balanced AND prefixes, target_delay sweeps (~40 variants total).

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Control moved off the multiply path**: only one product bit needed post-multiplier (v2) — biggest single win.
2. **Unified shift + single shifter/rounder** (v2→v5): `sh = max(11−lz, 26−s)` with early stage control and 1 late stage; `frac[i] = mp[i] ^ (guard & &mp[i-1:0])` (sticky drops out of all carries but bit 0).
3. **Mask-OR `d`** replacing a variable-index extraction (−1 level + area).
4. **Flat exponent SOP + narrow funnel shifter + merged `u0`** (v17/v41): −3.4% area each with no delay cost.

### 3. Regressions / what didn't work
- **Normalized-operand multiply (v4)**: CLZ+shifters moved ~240 ps *in front* of the multiplier — worse despite eliminating the `d` tree.
- **AND-form fraction gating** (v22–v27): consistently +85 ps across 6/6 runs.
- **`E0v±1` candidate form** (v20–v26): ~+100 ps systematic penalty.
- **Nested exponent muxes** (v40, v24): ~+130 ps vs flat SOP.
- `casex` CLZ (v39) failed lint (X-literals banned).
- target_delay: 300/500 gave identical netlists; 1200/985 slightly worse ADP — no benefit.

### 4. Lessons learned
- ABC/Yosys mapping noise is ±5%; some source forms (AND-tree grouping, sticky/rnd form, SOP vs AND-OR) normalize to identical netlists — I burned steps re-rolling neutral knobs before discovering this via bit-identical results (v29==v30, v19==v35).
- Better process: first identify which source changes actually alter the post-opt netlist (compare results for bit-identical ADP), then sweep only real knobs, and isolate systematic effects across paired runs instead of chasing single draws.
- Delay/area "families" are real: once a config's characteristic delay is known (~970 ps here), only systematic area cuts (like merged `u0`) improve ADP.
